# RESULTS vssT: verification of THEORY_vss2.md

Generated by `python vssT.py report` from out/vssT_*.json.
All dB values: more negative is better (L1 = ANR mean in dB, L2 = 10log10(sum e^2/sum d^2), ex = 10log10 of the mean Phi-excess v'Phi v/Jo over the window).

## E1: one-step oracle (Sec. 7 item 1)

mu_or: argmin of the true Phi-excess after one step from the current state with the block's own direction (Delta units, w <- w - mu Delta). ens(u): frozen-state ensemble over K blocks, E<v,u>_Phi / E|u|^2_Phi with u = Delta/max(g,g_min), the units of mu_hat. mu_hat columns: Euclidean C,Q, f=0.9, tau_m=2.5, tau_a=3.8, a0 = 1 or tau_a; mu in the formula = mu_form (the rule's mu state / fixed mu). ref wM: v = w - wM; ref fp: v relative to the method's model fixed point.

## E2: correlation times (Sec. 7 item 2)

Two-sided correlation sums |lag| <= 12 of zeta (direction from the optimum residual, mean removed) and of the fluctuation H~v (direction from the coherent error r*v minus its time mean); cross = sum_l E<zeta_n, H~v_{n+l}> / sqrt(N S). f = S / (h^2 |v|^2) with h = 0.25; h_eff = <mean, v>/|v|^2. E = Euclidean, P = Phi.

## E3: gain / participation-ratio law (Sec. 7 item 3, Sec. 3)

Frozen probes. noiseP: |Delta|^2_Phi/Jo with w at the model fixed point (pure noise). sigP: <v, Delta_c>_Phi/|v|^2_Phi for v = -w_fp (w = 0), Delta_c from the coherent error. Theory: noise/g^2 ~ (1 - pi), sig/g ~ h = 0.25.
